2026-11-03T10:52:53.6996385Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve PP4 round 27 with the Prefill polling backoff and norm square FMA behind independent gates after greedy

Round 27 tests two candidates from the power-cap analysis. Prefill runs at
the 700 W cap with SM clock near 1.4 GHz, so less energy for the same work
should raise clock. Each candidate has its own in-job gate on all twelve
Prefill GPUs and is adopted independently. Failure, missing results or
timeout leave the current path in place.

A. MegaMoE global polling backoff, DG_SM90_PREFILL_POLL_BACKOFF=64. After
three failed checks, the L1 and L2 arrival waits, the grid sync bit, the
NVLink signal and the expert count poll sleep for 64 ns. mbarrier waits
are unchanged because they already suspend. The output must be byte exact,
historical routing must improve by max(5 us, 0.5 percent), and hot and
balanced must not regress.

B. norm_quant square sum by explicit FMA, SGLANG_PREFILL_NORM_SQUARE_FMA=1.
Only the sixteen-element square sum changes, which changes FP32 order and
nothing else. Review relaxed the FP8 code count against the FP64
reference: strict non-regression would reject a tie flip at an FP8
rounding boundary, and the CPU model went from 11664 to 11665 out of 32.7
million codes. The count may now rise by max(8, control/1000). Maximum
error stays strictly non-increasing.

Budget. Round 26 ran its gate ahead of the Decode greedy gate, and greedy
then skipped for lack of budget. Round 27 runs after greedy. Round 26 took
52 s of wall time for one gate. This run adds a second gate and a cold
Triton compile, so review widened the budget from 60 and 70 s to 90 s per
worker and 100 s including aggregation. The gate starts only when SECONDS
plus 100 plus 660 is at most 1020, which allows a start up to 260 s. Greedy
finished at 222 to 236 s in round 25.

Both gates print a once-per-process dispatch line, and the sbatch collects
these per worker into results/prefill-r27-dispatch-N.txt. A needs a fresh
build because it changes the DeepGEMM host; B is a provisioning overlay.
The pin stays at a001f901 until a winning build is pinned. The full suite
and ruff pass.
